# Health Cloud Original Certification-Style Q&A

These are original practice questions for preparation. They are not copied exam dumps.

## Foundation

### Q1. [troubleshooting] How should a Salesforce professional handle consent for healthcare and life-sciences Salesforce implementation?

**Answer:** For Health Cloud, the correct approach is to connect consent to the business requirement, the Salesforce data model, security, limits, and operational support. In a healthcare and life-sciences Salesforce implementation scenario, start by clarifying volume, users, permissions, integration boundaries, and failure handling. For support, inspect recent deployments, debug logs, failed flow interviews, integration logs, record access, validation rules, and representative data samples before changing metadata.

**Interview Follow-Up:** Describe one risk, one test case, and one monitoring signal for this answer.
